### The KL Divergence Is Never Negative, Except in PyTorch

A Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence is never negative, yet PyTorch users have reported negative values for years, and the standard advice is to clamp them at zero. The sign is only the visible part of a silent failure.

*Fontes: [Hacker News](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30835>)*
